# Milestone 2 Mutation API Matrix

Generated from public headers by `docs/milestone2_mutation_api_matrix.py`.
This file is the freeze snapshot for roadmap item 1: every row is a public mutation-like API entrypoint discovered in the current `dev` working tree.

Evidence status is intentionally conservative: `UNPROVEN` means the API is in the frozen matrix but still needs or already relies on a dedicated bounded allocation/pre-state test before it may be promoted to `PASS`.

Total mutation-like public API rows: `824`.
